{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wiz6jT2BqbJc21iCyeE7bp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Call onError for a rejection that is not an Error\n2\t\n3\tIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#95\n4\tLabels: priority:medium, type:bug, size:S, area:api\n5\t\n6\t## Why\n7\t\n8\tThe orchestrator's `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error`. A rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string, a plain object, a value from a library that rejects with its own shape — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n9\t\n10\t`onError` is the seam a deployer uses to count failures, page someone, or write an incident record. A gate that silently drops a class of failures makes it a liar in exactly the cases nobody tested for, and the deployer's dashboard reads \"no errors\" while the caller sees rejections.\n11\t\n12\tRecorded as measured behaviour in `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` by HALEngine#83, which scoped itself to specifying the seam rather than changing it, and raised again by the reviewer on that PR. The spec notes this property is owned by no issue in the epic; this is that issue.\n13\t\n14\t## What to do\n15\t\n16\tCall `onError` for every rejection. Whatever is not already an `Error` gets wrapped in one, preserving the original as the `cause`, so a hook typed against `Error` keeps compiling and a hook that wants the raw value can still reach it. The re-thrown value at the caller boundary stays exactly what it is today, so nothing downstream changes shape.\n17\t\n18\t## Acceptance criteria\n19\t\n20\t- [ ] A provider rejecting with a string calls `onError` once, with an `Error` whose `cause` is that string, and the caller still receives the original rejection unchanged\n21\t- [ ] The same holds for a rejection with a plain object and with `undefined`\n22\t- [ ] A rejection that is already an `Error` reaches `onError` as the same instance, not a wrapper, pinned by the existing tests remaining unedited\n23\t- [ ] `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` replaces its recorded-flaw line with the new guarantee, and the CHANGELOG says what changes for an existing consumer\n24\t- [ ] `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run eslint` (`--max-warnings 0`) and `npm run prettier:check` pass\n25\t\n26\t## Tech notes\n27\t\n28\tKey files: `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` (the `catch` and its `instanceof Error` gate), `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md`.\n29\t\n30\tOut of scope: `beforeSession` escaping the `try` (its own issue) and usage accounting across tool rounds (HALEngine#93).\n31\t\n32\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a9a36594-97dc-4db3-ac1c-2f58243f1825","uuid":"7860f04d-0261-41c9-933a-e744f655da4b","timestamp":"2026-11-03T09:04:19.013Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/076263a3fce5/workspace/ticket.md","content":"# Call onError for a rejection that is not an Error\n\nIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#95\nLabels: priority:medium, type:bug, size:S, area:api\n\n## Why\n\nThe orchestrator's `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error`. A rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string, a plain object, a value from a library that rejects with its own shape — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n\n`onError` is the seam a deployer uses to count failures, page someone, or write an incident record. A gate that silently drops a class of failures makes it a liar in exactly the cases nobody tested for, and the deployer's dashboard reads \"no errors\" while the caller sees rejections.\n\nRecorded as measured behaviour in `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` by HALEngine#83, which scoped itself to specifying the seam rather than changing it, and raised again by the reviewer on that PR. The spec notes this property is owned by no issue in the epic; this is that issue.\n\n## What to do\n\nCall `onError` for every rejection. Whatever is not already an `Error` gets wrapped in one, preserving the original as the `cause`, so a hook typed against `Error` keeps compiling and a hook that wants the raw value can still reach it. The re-thrown value at the caller boundary stays exactly what it is today, so nothing downstream changes shape.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] A provider rejecting with a string calls `onError` once, with an `Error` whose `cause` is that string, and the caller still receives the original rejection unchanged\n- [ ] The same holds for a rejection with a plain object and with `undefined`\n- [ ] A rejection that is already an `Error` reaches `onError` as the same instance, not a wrapper, pinned by the existing tests remaining unedited\n- [ ] `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` replaces its recorded-flaw line with the new guarantee, and the CHANGELOG says what changes for an existing consumer\n- [ ] `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run eslint` (`--max-warnings 0`) and `npm run prettier:check` pass\n\n## Tech notes\n\nKey files: `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` (the `catch` and its `instanceof Error` gate), `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md`.\n\nOut of scope: `beforeSession` escaping the `try` (its own issue) and usage accounting across tool rounds (HALEngine#93).\n\n","numLines":32,"startLine":1,"totalLines":32}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01Wiz6jT2BqbJc21iCyeE7bp","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YKdM1ypkecDYRfmgRWa3Xx","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Definition of Done\n2\t\n3\t> The orchestrator's `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error`. A rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string, a plain object, a response body rejected directly by an HTTP client — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n4\t\n5\t**Strategy: `direct`** — the seam already exists. `createChatOrchestrator` returns an orchestrator whose `processMessage` and `processMessageStream` invoke the installed `OrchestratorHooks`. A test can drive `processMessage` with a provider that throws a string or a plain object and observe that `onError` is bypassed today because of the `error instanceof Error` gate in the `catch` block.\n6\t\n7\t## Done when these pass\n8\t\n9\t- [ ] **calls onError with an Error carrying the thrown string and runs afterSession while rethrowing** — when a turn rejects with a string (`'string boom'`), `onError` is called once with an `Error` instance whose `message` is `'string boom'`, `afterSession` runs after `onError`, and the rejection still propagates to the caller. Fails today because `onError` is skipped (`callOrder` is `['afterSession']` and `isError` is `false`).\n10\t      `src/orchestration/onErrorNonError.acceptance.test.ts`\n11\t\n12\t- [ ] **calls onError with an Error carrying a readable message when the stream rejects with a plain object** — when a turn rejects with a plain object (`{code: 503, detail: 'upstream down'}`), `onError` is called once with an `Error` instance carrying a non-empty `message`, `afterSession` runs after `onError`, and the rejection still propagates to the caller. Fails today because `onError` is skipped (`callOrder` is `['afterSession']` and `isError` is `false`).\n13\t      `src/orchestration/onErrorNonError.acceptance.test.ts`\n14\t\n15\t## Facets\n16\t\n17\t- [ ] In `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts`, coerce a caught non-`Error` rejection to an `Error` with a readable `message` (while passing an `Error` instance through unchanged) so `hooks.onError` always fires on any rejection inside the `try` block.\n18\t- [ ] Confirm existing `Error`-instance tests in `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts` (`called with session and error when stream fails`, `error still propagates after onError hook`, `error path calls onError then afterSession`) remain green without modification.\n19\t- [ ] Add a `CHANGELOG.md` entry under `## [Unreleased]` describing the behaviour change for existing consumers.\n20\t\n21\t## Out of scope\n22\t\n23\t- `beforeSession` escaping the `try` block (resolved separately in HALEngine#94).\n24\t- Usage accounting across tool rounds (resolved separately in HALEngine#93).\n25\t- Widening the `onError` hook parameter type away from `Error` (the ticket keeps `(session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>`).\n26\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a9a36594-97dc-4db3-ac1c-2f58243f1825","uuid":"7bc593e0-7d4c-446d-b7a8-d793b0476faa","timestamp":"2026-11-03T09:04:19.396Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/076263a3fce5/workspace/target/.lore/dod.md","content":"# Definition of Done\n\n> The orchestrator's `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error`. A rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string, a plain object, a response body rejected directly by an HTTP client — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n\n**Strategy: `direct`** — the seam already exists. `createChatOrchestrator` returns an orchestrator whose `processMessage` and `processMessageStream` invoke the installed `OrchestratorHooks`. A test can drive `processMessage` with a provider that throws a string or a plain object and observe that `onError` is bypassed today because of the `error instanceof Error` gate in the `catch` block.\n\n## Done when these pass\n\n- [ ] **calls onError with an Error carrying the thrown string and runs afterSession while rethrowing** — when a turn rejects with a string (`'string boom'`), `onError` is called once with an `Error` instance whose `message` is `'string boom'`, `afterSession` runs after `onError`, and the rejection still propagates to the caller. Fails today because `onError` is skipped (`callOrder` is `['afterSession']` and `isError` is `false`).\n      `src/orchestration/onErrorNonError.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **calls onError with an Error carrying a readable message when the stream rejects with a plain object** — when a turn rejects with a plain object (`{code: 503, detail: 'upstream down'}`), `onError` is called once with an `Error` instance carrying a non-empty `message`, `afterSession` runs after `onError`, and the rejection still propagates to the caller. Fails today because `onError` is skipped (`callOrder` is `['afterSession']` and `isError` is `false`).\n      `src/orchestration/onErrorNonError.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n## Facets\n\n- [ ] In `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts`, coerce a caught non-`Error` rejection to an `Error` with a readable `message` (while passing an `Error` instance through unchanged) so `hooks.onError` always fires on any rejection inside the `try` block.\n- [ ] Confirm existing `Error`-instance tests in `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts` (`called with session and error when stream fails`, `error still propagates after onError hook`, `error path calls onError then afterSession`) remain green without modification.\n- [ ] Add a `CHANGELOG.md` entry under `## [Unreleased]` describing the behaviour change for existing consumers.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- `beforeSession` escaping the `try` block (resolved separately in HALEngine#94).\n- Usage accounting across tool rounds (resolved separately in HALEngine#93).\n- Widening the `onError` hook parameter type away from `Error` (the ticket keeps `(session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>`).\n","numLines":26,"startLine":1,"totalLines":26}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01YKdM1ypkecDYRfmgRWa3Xx","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FQ9uqmh8p6QiXmX4bi3xrU","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\n2\timport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\n3\timport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\n4\timport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\n5\timport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n6\t\n7\tconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n8\t\n9\tconst sessionWith = (content: string): ChatSession => ({\n10\t  sessionId: 'test-session',\n11\t  userId: 'user-1',\n12\t  entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: new Date().toISOString()}],\n13\t});\n14\t\n15\tconst rejectingWith = (reason: unknown): AIProvider => ({\n16\t  // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n17\t  async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n18\t    throw reason;\n19\t  },\n20\t  async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n21\t    return {} as T;\n22\t  },\n23\t});\n24\t\n25\tconst turnOutcome = async (reason: unknown) => {\n26\t  const callOrder: string[] = [];\n27\t  let seenError: Error | undefined;\n28\t  const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n29\t    onError: async (_session, error) => {\n30\t      callOrder.push('onError');\n31\t      seenError = error;\n32\t    },\n33\t    afterSession: async () => {\n34\t      callOrder.push('afterSession');\n35\t    },\n36\t  };\n37\t\n38\t  const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(rejectingWith(reason), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n39\t  const outcome = await orchestrator.processMessage(sessionWith('hello')).then(\n40\t    value => ({rejected: false, reason: value as unknown}),\n41\t    (thrown: unknown) => ({rejected: true, reason: thrown})\n42\t  );\n43\t\n44\t  return {\n45\t    outcome,\n46\t    callOrder,\n47\t    isError: seenError instanceof Error,\n48\t    message: seenError?.message,\n49\t  };\n50\t};\n51\t\n52\tdescribe('ChatOrchestrator onError on non-Error rejections', () => {\n53\t  it('calls onError with an Error carrying the thrown string and runs afterSession while rethrowing', async () => {\n54\t    const result = await turnOutcome('string boom');\n55\t\n56\t    expect(result).toEqual({\n57\t      outcome: {rejected: true, reason: 'string boom'},\n58\t      callOrder: ['onError', 'afterSession'],\n59\t      isError: true,\n60\t      message: 'string boom',\n61\t    });\n62\t  });\n63\t\n64\t  it('calls onError with an Error carrying a readable message when the stream rejects with a plain object', async () => {\n65\t    const reason = {code: 503, detail: 'upstream down'};\n66\t\n67\t    const result = await turnOutcome(reason);\n68\t\n69\t    expect({\n70\t      outcome: result.outcome,\n71\t      callOrder: result.callOrder,\n72\t      isError: result.isError,\n73\t      hasMessage: typeof result.message === 'string' && result.message.length > 0,\n74\t    }).toEqual({\n75\t      outcome: {rejected: true, reason},\n76\t      callOrder: ['onError', 'afterSession'],\n77\t      isError: true,\n78\t      hasMessage: true,\n79\t    });\n80\t  });\n81\t});\n82\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a9a36594-97dc-4db3-ac1c-2f58243f1825","uuid":"25dddf4d-bcde-4793-bf3a-38de1e256ec1","timestamp":"2026-11-03T09:04:30.828Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/076263a3fce5/workspace/target/src/orchestration/onErrorNonError.acceptance.test.ts","content":"import {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\nconst sessionWith = (content: string): ChatSession => ({\n  sessionId: 'test-session',\n  userId: 'user-1',\n  entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: new Date().toISOString()}],\n});\n\nconst rejectingWith = (reason: unknown): AIProvider => ({\n  // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n  async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    throw reason;\n  },\n  async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n    return {} as T;\n  },\n});\n\nconst turnOutcome = async (reason: unknown) => {\n  const callOrder: string[] = [];\n  let seenError: Error | undefined;\n  const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n    onError: async (_session, error) => {\n      callOrder.push('onError');\n      seenError = error;\n    },\n    afterSession: async () => {\n      callOrder.push('afterSession');\n    },\n  };\n\n  const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(rejectingWith(reason), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n  const outcome = await orchestrator.processMessage(sessionWith('hello')).then(\n    value => ({rejected: false, reason: value as unknown}),\n    (thrown: unknown) => ({rejected: true, reason: thrown})\n  );\n\n  return {\n    outcome,\n    callOrder,\n    isError: seenError instanceof Error,\n    message: seenError?.message,\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator onError on non-Error rejections', () => {\n  it('calls onError with an Error carrying the thrown string and runs afterSession while rethrowing', async () => {\n    const result = await turnOutcome('string boom');\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({\n      outcome: {rejected: true, reason: 'string boom'},\n      callOrder: ['onError', 'afterSession'],\n      isError: true,\n      message: 'string boom',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('calls onError with an Error carrying a readable message when the stream rejects with a plain object', async () => {\n    const reason = {code: 503, detail: 'upstream down'};\n\n    const result = await turnOutcome(reason);\n\n    expect({\n      outcome: result.outcome,\n      callOrder: result.callOrder,\n      isError: result.isError,\n      hasMessage: typeof result.message === 'string' && result.message.length > 0,\n    }).toEqual({\n      outcome: {rejected: true, reason},\n      callOrder: ['onError', 'afterSession'],\n      isError: true,\n      hasMessage: true,\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":82,"startLine":1,"totalLines":82}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01FQ9uqmh8p6QiXmX4bi3xrU","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01STQSQdLd2poq2FSi5Nv5HG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\n2\timport type {ToolRegistry, ToolContext, ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\n3\timport type {\n4\t  AIProvider,\n5\t  Message,\n6\t  MessageChunk,\n7\t  SendMessageParams,\n8\t  ToolCall,\n9\t  ToolResultContent,\n10\t  UsageMetadata,\n11\t} from '../types/ai.js';\n12\timport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\n13\timport type {PromptBuilder, PromptBuilderOptions} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n14\timport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n15\timport {toMessages, createContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\n16\timport type {ContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\n17\timport {\n18\t  shouldContinueToolLoop,\n19\t  collectText,\n20\t  buildToolCallMessages,\n21\t  collectToolCalls,\n22\t  extractStopReason,\n23\t  extractUsage,\n24\t} from './orchestratorHelpers.js';\n25\timport {appendEntry, commitStreamingEntries} from './entryMutations.js';\n26\timport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n27\t\n28\tconst DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS = 5;\n29\t\n30\texport interface OrchestratorHooks {\n31\t  beforeSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n32\t  afterSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n33\t  beforeUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>;\n34\t  afterUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<void>;\n35\t  beforeModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, systemPrompt: string) => Promise<string>;\n36\t  afterModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, responseText: string, totalUsage?: UsageMetadata) => Promise<void>;\n37\t  onError?: (session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>;\n38\t  /** The supported seam for an EU AI Act Art. 14 human-oversight control: fires before each known tool's executor, and a returned ToolResponse replaces the call (see docs/adding-a-tool.md); it receives the model's raw tool input and, through session, the caller's authHeaders, so a policy that logs either logs personal data and credential material; the engine asserts nothing about any policy installed here. */\n39\t  beforeToolCall?: (session: ChatSession, call: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse | undefined>;\n40\t  /** Fires when the model asks for a tool round the budget refuses, after the last provider call and before afterModelResponse; a returned string reaches the user as the turn's closing text, and the engine writes none of its own (see specs/hal-engine-tool-budget/spec.md). */\n41\t  onToolBudgetExhausted?: (session: ChatSession, budget: ToolBudgetInfo) => Promise<string | undefined>;\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\texport interface ToolBudgetInfo {\n45\t  maxToolRounds: number;\n46\t  requestedTools: string[];\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\ttype BeforeToolCall = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['beforeToolCall']>;\n50\ttype OnToolBudgetExhausted = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['onToolBudgetExhausted']>;\n51\t\n52\texport const TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED = 'tool_budget_exhausted';\n53\t\n54\texport interface ChatOrchestrator {\n55\t  processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string>;\n56\t  processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk>;\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\texport interface ChatOrchestratorOptions {\n60\t  maxToolRounds?: number;\n61\t  contextConfig?: Partial<ContextConfig>;\n62\t  promptBuilderOptions?: PromptBuilderOptions;\n63\t  hooks?: OrchestratorHooks;\n64\t  sessionStore?: SessionStore;\n65\t}\n66\t\n67\tinterface ToolExecutionResult {\n68\t  clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[];\n69\t  suppressOutput: boolean;\n70\t}\n71\texport function createChatOrchestrator(\n72\t  provider: AIProvider,\n73\t  promptBuilder: PromptBuilder,\n74\t  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry,\n75\t  options?: ChatOrchestratorOptions\n76\t): ChatOrchestrator {\n77\t  const maxToolRounds = normalizeToolRounds(options?.maxToolRounds);\n78\t  const contextConfig = createContextConfig(options?.contextConfig);\n79\t  const toolInstructions = toolRegistry?.getPromptInstructions();\n80\t  const baseSystemPrompt = promptBuilder.build({...options?.promptBuilderOptions, toolInstructions});\n81\t  const tools = toolRegistry?.getDefinitions();\n82\t  const hooks = options?.hooks;\n83\t  const sessionStore = options?.sessionStore;\n84\t\n85\t  return {\n86\t    async processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string> {\n87\t      return collectText(this.processMessageStream(session));\n88\t    },\n89\t\n90\t    async *processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n91\t      try {\n92\t        if (hooks?.beforeSession) await hooks.beforeSession(session);\n93\t\n94\t        let userMessage = lastUserContent(session);\n95\t\n96\t        if (hooks?.beforeUserInput) {\n97\t          userMessage = await rewriteUserMessage(session, userMessage, hooks.beforeUserInput);\n98\t        }\n99\t\n100\t        if (hooks?.afterUserInput) await hooks.afterUserInput(session, userMessage);\n101\t\n102\t        const systemPrompt = hooks?.beforeModelResponse\n103\t          ? await hooks.beforeModelResponse(session, baseSystemPrompt)\n104\t          : baseSystemPrompt;\n105\t\n106\t        const messages: Message[] = toMessages(session.entries, contextConfig);\n107\t        let totalUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n108\t        let responseText = '';\n109\t\n110\t        // Bounded by the budget gate below, not the header: maxToolRounds executed rounds, maxToolRounds + 1 provider calls.\n111\t        for (let round = 0; ; round++) {\n112\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'starting round', {round});\n113\t          const pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = [];\n114\t\n115\t          const outcome = yield* streamRound(provider, {messages, systemPrompt, tools}, pendingToolCalls);\n116\t          responseText += outcome.text;\n117\t          totalUsage = accumulateUsage(totalUsage, outcome.usage);\n118\t\n119\t          if (!shouldContinueToolLoop(outcome.stopReason, pendingToolCalls, toolRegistry)) break;\n120\t          if (budgetSpent(round, maxToolRounds, pendingToolCalls)) {\n121\t            const budget = {maxToolRounds, requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)};\n122\t            responseText += yield* closeExhaustedTurn(session, budget, hooks?.onToolBudgetExhausted);\n123\t            break;\n124\t          }\n125\t\n126\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'executing tools', {tools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)});\n127\t          const {clientMessages, suppressOutput} = await executeToolCalls(\n128\t            pendingToolCalls,\n129\t            messages,\n130\t            session,\n131\t            toolCallRunner(toolRegistry!, session, hooks?.beforeToolCall)\n132\t          );\n133\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'tool execution complete', {\n134\t            clientMessageCount: clientMessages.length,\n135\t            clientMessageTypes: clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n136\t            suppressOutput,\n137\t          });\n138\t\n139\t          if (clientMessages.length > 0) {\n140\t            yield {type: 'tool_result' as const, clientMessages};\n141\t          }\n142\t\n143\t          if (suppressOutput) {\n144\t            yield {type: 'suppress_output' as const};\n145\t          }\n146\t        }\n147\t\n148\t        if (hooks?.afterModelResponse) await hooks.afterModelResponse(session, responseText, totalUsage);\n149\t      } catch (error) {\n150\t        if (hooks?.onError && error instanceof Error) {\n151\t          await hooks.onError(session, error);\n152\t        }\n153\t        throw error;\n154\t      } finally {\n155\t        // Before both: a provider that threw mid-stream leaves an entry open, and nothing should persist it that way.\n156\t        commitStreamingEntries(session);\n157\t        try {\n158\t          if (hooks?.afterSession) await hooks.afterSession(session);\n159\t        } finally {\n160\t          await saveSession(sessionStore, session);\n161\t        }\n162\t      }\n163\t    },\n164\t  };\n165\t}\n166\t\n167\t// A negative budget means no tool rounds; a non-finite one, NaN included, falls back to the default rather than never ending.\n168\tfunction normalizeToolRounds(value: number | undefined): number {\n169\t  if (value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value)) return DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS;\n170\t  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));\n171\t}\n172\t\n173\t// Gates execution, not the provider call: a round nobody can read is never run, and the call that read the last one already has.\n174\tfunction budgetSpent(round: number, maxToolRounds: number, pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]): boolean {\n175\t  if (round < maxToolRounds) return false;\n176\t  log.warn('orchestrator', 'tool budget exhausted', {\n177\t    maxToolRounds,\n178\t    requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name),\n179\t  });\n180\t  return true;\n181\t}\n182\t\n183\t// The stop always follows the text: the round's own stop already committed its entry, and a bare text chunk would open one never committed.\n184\tasync function* closeExhaustedTurn(\n185\t  session: ChatSession,\n186\t  budget: ToolBudgetInfo,\n187\t  hook?: OnToolBudgetExhausted\n188\t): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, string> {\n189\t  const sentence = (hook ? await hook(session, budget) : undefined) ?? '';\n190\t  if (sentence !== '') yield {type: 'text', text: sentence};\n191\t  yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED};\n192\t  return sentence;\n193\t}\n194\t\n195\tinterface RoundOutcome {\n196\t  stopReason: string;\n197\t  text: string;\n198\t  usage?: UsageMetadata;\n199\t}\n200\t\n201\t// `yield*` in the caller evaluates to this return, so the round's totals come back as data.\n202\tasync function* streamRound(\n203\t  provider: AIProvider,\n204\t  request: SendMessageParams,\n205\t  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]\n206\t): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, RoundOutcome> {\n207\t  let stopReason = '';\n208\t  let text = '';\n209\t  let usage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n210\t\n211\t  for await (const chunk of provider.sendMessage(request)) {\n212\t    yield chunk;\n213\t    collectToolCalls(chunk, pendingToolCalls);\n214\t    stopReason = extractStopReason(chunk) ?? stopReason;\n215\t    usage = extractUsage(chunk) ?? usage;\n216\t    if (chunk.type === 'text') text += chunk.text;\n217\t  }\n218\t\n219\t  return {stopReason, text, usage};\n220\t}\n221\t\n222\t// A changed message is also written back to the session's last user entry.\n223\tasync function rewriteUserMessage(\n224\t  session: ChatSession,\n225\t  userMessage: string,\n226\t  hook: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>\n227\t): Promise<string> {\n228\t  const modified = await hook(session, userMessage);\n229\t  if (modified === userMessage) return userMessage;\n230\t\n231\t  updateLastUserContent(session, modified);\n232\t  return modified;\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\tfunction lastUserContent(session: ChatSession): string {\n236\t  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n237\t    const entry = session.entries[i];\n238\t    if (entry.role === 'user') return entry.content;\n239\t  }\n240\t  return '';\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\tfunction updateLastUserContent(session: ChatSession, content: string): void {\n244\t  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n245\t    const entry = session.entries[i];\n246\t    if (entry.role === 'user') {\n247\t      entry.content = content;\n248\t      return;\n249\t    }\n250\t  }\n251\t}\n252\t\n253\t// One runner per round: the policy sees each known call before its executor, and an unknown name keeps the registry's own path.\n254\tfunction toolCallRunner(toolRegistry: ToolRegistry, session: ChatSession, beforeToolCall?: BeforeToolCall) {\n255\t  const context: ToolContext = {\n256\t    userId: session.userId,\n257\t    sessionId: session.sessionId,\n258\t    workspaceId: session.workspaceId,\n259\t    authHeaders: session.authHeaders,\n260\t  };\n261\t\n262\t  return async (tc: ToolCall): Promise<ToolResponse> => {\n263\t    const declined =\n264\t      beforeToolCall && toolRegistry.has(tc.name) ? await consultPolicy(beforeToolCall, session, tc) : undefined;\n265\t    return declined ?? toolRegistry.execute(tc.name, tc.input, context);\n266\t  };\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\t// A throwing policy declines its call rather than failing the turn: every tool_use must still be answered.\n270\tasync function consultPolicy(\n271\t  policy: BeforeToolCall,\n272\t  session: ChatSession,\n273\t  tc: ToolCall\n274\t): Promise<ToolResponse | undefined> {\n275\t  try {\n276\t    return await policy(session, tc);\n277\t  } catch (error) {\n278\t    log.warn('orchestrator', 'beforeToolCall threw, so the call is declined', {\n279\t      tool: tc.name,\n280\t      errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error,\n281\t    });\n282\t    return {result: `The ${tc.name} call was declined and not performed.`};\n283\t  }\n284\t}\n285\t\n286\tasync function executeToolCalls(\n287\t  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[],\n288\t  messages: Message[],\n289\t  session: ChatSession,\n290\t  runToolCall: (tc: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse>\n291\t): Promise<ToolExecutionResult> {\n292\t  messages.push(buildToolCallMessages(pendingToolCalls));\n293\t\n294\t  const responses = await Promise.all(\n295\t    pendingToolCalls.map(async tc => {\n296\t      const response = await runToolCall(tc);\n297\t      return {tc, response};\n298\t    })\n299\t  );\n300\t\n301\t  const toolResults: ToolResultContent[] = responses.map(({tc, response}) => ({\n302\t    type: 'tool_result' as const,\n303\t    toolUseId: tc.id,\n304\t    content: response.result,\n305\t  }));\n306\t  messages.push({role: 'user', content: toolResults});\n307\t\n308\t  const rawClientMessages = responses.flatMap(({response}) => response.clientMessages ?? []);\n309\t\n310\t  return {\n311\t    clientMessages: assignEntryIndices(rawClientMessages, session),\n312\t    suppressOutput: responses.some(({response}) => response.suppressAssistantResponse === true),\n313\t  };\n314\t}\n315\t\n316\tfunction assignEntryIndices(clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[], session: ChatSession): OutgoingMessage[] {\n317\t  return clientMessages.map(msg => {\n318\t    if (msg.type !== 'entry_upsert') return msg;\n319\t    const index = appendEntry(session, msg.entry);\n320\t    return {...msg, index};\n321\t  });\n322\t}\n323\t\n324\t// Adds per-round token counts so the hook receives the total across all provider calls in the turn.\n325\tfunction accumulateUsage(acc: UsageMetadata | undefined, next: UsageMetadata | undefined): UsageMetadata | undefined {\n326\t  if (!acc) return next;\n327\t  if (!next) return acc;\n328\t  return {\n329\t    inputTokens: acc.inputTokens + next.inputTokens,\n330\t    outputTokens: acc.outputTokens + next.outputTokens,\n331\t    totalTokens: acc.totalTokens + next.totalTokens,\n332\t  };\n333\t}\n334\t\n335\t// A store outage is not a reason to fail the turn the customer already received.\n336\tasync function saveSession(store: SessionStore | undefined, session: ChatSession): Promise<void> {\n337\t  if (!store?.save) return;\n338\t\n339\t  try {\n340\t    await store.save(session);\n341\t  } catch (error) {\n342\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n343\t    log.error('orchestrator', 'session save failed', {sessionId: session.sessionId, error: message});\n344\t  }\n345\t}\n346\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a9a36594-97dc-4db3-ac1c-2f58243f1825","uuid":"02096d14-9a89-4b09-88e4-2a4d695ab97f","timestamp":"2026-11-03T09:04:33.959Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/076263a3fce5/workspace/target/src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts","content":"import type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {ToolRegistry, ToolContext, ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {\n  AIProvider,\n  Message,\n  MessageChunk,\n  SendMessageParams,\n  ToolCall,\n  ToolResultContent,\n  UsageMetadata,\n} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {PromptBuilder, PromptBuilderOptions} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {toMessages, createContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\nimport type {ContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\nimport {\n  shouldContinueToolLoop,\n  collectText,\n  buildToolCallMessages,\n  collectToolCalls,\n  extractStopReason,\n  extractUsage,\n} from './orchestratorHelpers.js';\nimport {appendEntry, commitStreamingEntries} from './entryMutations.js';\nimport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\nconst DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS = 5;\n\nexport interface OrchestratorHooks {\n  beforeSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n  afterSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n  beforeUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>;\n  afterUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<void>;\n  beforeModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, systemPrompt: string) => Promise<string>;\n  afterModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, responseText: string, totalUsage?: UsageMetadata) => Promise<void>;\n  onError?: (session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>;\n  /** The supported seam for an EU AI Act Art. 14 human-oversight control: fires before each known tool's executor, and a returned ToolResponse replaces the call (see docs/adding-a-tool.md); it receives the model's raw tool input and, through session, the caller's authHeaders, so a policy that logs either logs personal data and credential material; the engine asserts nothing about any policy installed here. */\n  beforeToolCall?: (session: ChatSession, call: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse | undefined>;\n  /** Fires when the model asks for a tool round the budget refuses, after the last provider call and before afterModelResponse; a returned string reaches the user as the turn's closing text, and the engine writes none of its own (see specs/hal-engine-tool-budget/spec.md). */\n  onToolBudgetExhausted?: (session: ChatSession, budget: ToolBudgetInfo) => Promise<string | undefined>;\n}\n\nexport interface ToolBudgetInfo {\n  maxToolRounds: number;\n  requestedTools: string[];\n}\n\ntype BeforeToolCall = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['beforeToolCall']>;\ntype OnToolBudgetExhausted = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['onToolBudgetExhausted']>;\n\nexport const TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED = 'tool_budget_exhausted';\n\nexport interface ChatOrchestrator {\n  processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string>;\n  processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk>;\n}\n\nexport interface ChatOrchestratorOptions {\n  maxToolRounds?: number;\n  contextConfig?: Partial<ContextConfig>;\n  promptBuilderOptions?: PromptBuilderOptions;\n  hooks?: OrchestratorHooks;\n  sessionStore?: SessionStore;\n}\n\ninterface ToolExecutionResult {\n  clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[];\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n}\nexport function createChatOrchestrator(\n  provider: AIProvider,\n  promptBuilder: PromptBuilder,\n  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry,\n  options?: ChatOrchestratorOptions\n): ChatOrchestrator {\n  const maxToolRounds = normalizeToolRounds(options?.maxToolRounds);\n  const contextConfig = createContextConfig(options?.contextConfig);\n  const toolInstructions = toolRegistry?.getPromptInstructions();\n  const baseSystemPrompt = promptBuilder.build({...options?.promptBuilderOptions, toolInstructions});\n  const tools = toolRegistry?.getDefinitions();\n  const hooks = options?.hooks;\n  const sessionStore = options?.sessionStore;\n\n  return {\n    async processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string> {\n      return collectText(this.processMessageStream(session));\n    },\n\n    async *processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      try {\n        if (hooks?.beforeSession) await hooks.beforeSession(session);\n\n        let userMessage = lastUserContent(session);\n\n        if (hooks?.beforeUserInput) {\n          userMessage = await rewriteUserMessage(session, userMessage, hooks.beforeUserInput);\n        }\n\n        if (hooks?.afterUserInput) await hooks.afterUserInput(session, userMessage);\n\n        const systemPrompt = hooks?.beforeModelResponse\n          ? await hooks.beforeModelResponse(session, baseSystemPrompt)\n          : baseSystemPrompt;\n\n        const messages: Message[] = toMessages(session.entries, contextConfig);\n        let totalUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n        let responseText = '';\n\n        // Bounded by the budget gate below, not the header: maxToolRounds executed rounds, maxToolRounds + 1 provider calls.\n        for (let round = 0; ; round++) {\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'starting round', {round});\n          const pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = [];\n\n          const outcome = yield* streamRound(provider, {messages, systemPrompt, tools}, pendingToolCalls);\n          responseText += outcome.text;\n          totalUsage = accumulateUsage(totalUsage, outcome.usage);\n\n          if (!shouldContinueToolLoop(outcome.stopReason, pendingToolCalls, toolRegistry)) break;\n          if (budgetSpent(round, maxToolRounds, pendingToolCalls)) {\n            const budget = {maxToolRounds, requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)};\n            responseText += yield* closeExhaustedTurn(session, budget, hooks?.onToolBudgetExhausted);\n            break;\n          }\n\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'executing tools', {tools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)});\n          const {clientMessages, suppressOutput} = await executeToolCalls(\n            pendingToolCalls,\n            messages,\n            session,\n            toolCallRunner(toolRegistry!, session, hooks?.beforeToolCall)\n          );\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'tool execution complete', {\n            clientMessageCount: clientMessages.length,\n            clientMessageTypes: clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n            suppressOutput,\n          });\n\n          if (clientMessages.length > 0) {\n            yield {type: 'tool_result' as const, clientMessages};\n          }\n\n          if (suppressOutput) {\n            yield {type: 'suppress_output' as const};\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (hooks?.afterModelResponse) await hooks.afterModelResponse(session, responseText, totalUsage);\n      } catch (error) {\n        if (hooks?.onError && error instanceof Error) {\n          await hooks.onError(session, error);\n        }\n        throw error;\n      } finally {\n        // Before both: a provider that threw mid-stream leaves an entry open, and nothing should persist it that way.\n        commitStreamingEntries(session);\n        try {\n          if (hooks?.afterSession) await hooks.afterSession(session);\n        } finally {\n          await saveSession(sessionStore, session);\n        }\n      }\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// A negative budget means no tool rounds; a non-finite one, NaN included, falls back to the default rather than never ending.\nfunction normalizeToolRounds(value: number | undefined): number {\n  if (value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value)) return DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS;\n  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));\n}\n\n// Gates execution, not the provider call: a round nobody can read is never run, and the call that read the last one already has.\nfunction budgetSpent(round: number, maxToolRounds: number, pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]): boolean {\n  if (round < maxToolRounds) return false;\n  log.warn('orchestrator', 'tool budget exhausted', {\n    maxToolRounds,\n    requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name),\n  });\n  return true;\n}\n\n// The stop always follows the text: the round's own stop already committed its entry, and a bare text chunk would open one never committed.\nasync function* closeExhaustedTurn(\n  session: ChatSession,\n  budget: ToolBudgetInfo,\n  hook?: OnToolBudgetExhausted\n): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, string> {\n  const sentence = (hook ? await hook(session, budget) : undefined) ?? '';\n  if (sentence !== '') yield {type: 'text', text: sentence};\n  yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED};\n  return sentence;\n}\n\ninterface RoundOutcome {\n  stopReason: string;\n  text: string;\n  usage?: UsageMetadata;\n}\n\n// `yield*` in the caller evaluates to this return, so the round's totals come back as data.\nasync function* streamRound(\n  provider: AIProvider,\n  request: SendMessageParams,\n  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]\n): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, RoundOutcome> {\n  let stopReason = '';\n  let text = '';\n  let usage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n\n  for await (const chunk of provider.sendMessage(request)) {\n    yield chunk;\n    collectToolCalls(chunk, pendingToolCalls);\n    stopReason = extractStopReason(chunk) ?? stopReason;\n    usage = extractUsage(chunk) ?? usage;\n    if (chunk.type === 'text') text += chunk.text;\n  }\n\n  return {stopReason, text, usage};\n}\n\n// A changed message is also written back to the session's last user entry.\nasync function rewriteUserMessage(\n  session: ChatSession,\n  userMessage: string,\n  hook: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>\n): Promise<string> {\n  const modified = await hook(session, userMessage);\n  if (modified === userMessage) return userMessage;\n\n  updateLastUserContent(session, modified);\n  return modified;\n}\n\nfunction lastUserContent(session: ChatSession): string {\n  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const entry = session.entries[i];\n    if (entry.role === 'user') return entry.content;\n  }\n  return '';\n}\n\nfunction updateLastUserContent(session: ChatSession, content: string): void {\n  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const entry = session.entries[i];\n    if (entry.role === 'user') {\n      entry.content = content;\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// One runner per round: the policy sees each known call before its executor, and an unknown name keeps the registry's own path.\nfunction toolCallRunner(toolRegistry: ToolRegistry, session: ChatSession, beforeToolCall?: BeforeToolCall) {\n  const context: ToolContext = {\n    userId: session.userId,\n    sessionId: session.sessionId,\n    workspaceId: session.workspaceId,\n    authHeaders: session.authHeaders,\n  };\n\n  return async (tc: ToolCall): Promise<ToolResponse> => {\n    const declined =\n      beforeToolCall && toolRegistry.has(tc.name) ? await consultPolicy(beforeToolCall, session, tc) : undefined;\n    return declined ?? toolRegistry.execute(tc.name, tc.input, context);\n  };\n}\n\n// A throwing policy declines its call rather than failing the turn: every tool_use must still be answered.\nasync function consultPolicy(\n  policy: BeforeToolCall,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  tc: ToolCall\n): Promise<ToolResponse | undefined> {\n  try {\n    return await policy(session, tc);\n  } catch (error) {\n    log.warn('orchestrator', 'beforeToolCall threw, so the call is declined', {\n      tool: tc.name,\n      errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error,\n    });\n    return {result: `The ${tc.name} call was declined and not performed.`};\n  }\n}\n\nasync function executeToolCalls(\n  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[],\n  messages: Message[],\n  session: ChatSession,\n  runToolCall: (tc: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse>\n): Promise<ToolExecutionResult> {\n  messages.push(buildToolCallMessages(pendingToolCalls));\n\n  const responses = await Promise.all(\n    pendingToolCalls.map(async tc => {\n      const response = await runToolCall(tc);\n      return {tc, response};\n    })\n  );\n\n  const toolResults: ToolResultContent[] = responses.map(({tc, response}) => ({\n    type: 'tool_result' as const,\n    toolUseId: tc.id,\n    content: response.result,\n  }));\n  messages.push({role: 'user', content: toolResults});\n\n  const rawClientMessages = responses.flatMap(({response}) => response.clientMessages ?? []);\n\n  return {\n    clientMessages: assignEntryIndices(rawClientMessages, session),\n    suppressOutput: responses.some(({response}) => response.suppressAssistantResponse === true),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction assignEntryIndices(clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[], session: ChatSession): OutgoingMessage[] {\n  return clientMessages.map(msg => {\n    if (msg.type !== 'entry_upsert') return msg;\n    const index = appendEntry(session, msg.entry);\n    return {...msg, index};\n  });\n}\n\n// Adds per-round token counts so the hook receives the total across all provider calls in the turn.\nfunction accumulateUsage(acc: UsageMetadata | undefined, next: UsageMetadata | undefined): UsageMetadata | undefined {\n  if (!acc) return next;\n  if (!next) return acc;\n  return {\n    inputTokens: acc.inputTokens + next.inputTokens,\n    outputTokens: acc.outputTokens + next.outputTokens,\n    totalTokens: acc.totalTokens + next.totalTokens,\n  };\n}\n\n// A store outage is not a reason to fail the turn the customer already received.\nasync function saveSession(store: SessionStore | undefined, session: ChatSession): Promise<void> {\n  if (!store?.save) return;\n\n  try {\n    await store.save(session);\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    log.error('orchestrator', 'session save failed', {sessionId: session.sessionId, error: message});\n  }\n}\n","numLines":346,"startLine":1,"totalLines":346}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01STQSQdLd2poq2FSi5Nv5HG","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, SendMessageParams, UsageMetadata} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\n\n// Fixed: nothing asserts on it, and new Date() would tie the run to the clock.\nconst SENT_AT = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';\n\n// One expect per test needs the rejection as a value rather than a matcher.\nconst outcomeOf = (call: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> =>\n  call.then(() => 'resolved without throwing').catch((error: Error) => error.message);\n\nfunction createSession(content: string): ChatSession {\n  return {\n    sessionId: 's1',\n    userId: 'u1',\n    entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: SENT_AT}],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createMockProvider(response: string, usage?: UsageMetadata): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    async *sendMessage(_params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      yield {type: 'text', text: response};\n      yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn', usage};\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ntype RecordedHook =\n  | 'beforeSession'\n  | 'beforeUserInput'\n  | 'afterUserInput'\n  | 'beforeModelResponse'\n  | 'afterModelResponse'\n  | 'afterSession'\n  | 'onError';\n\n// Only the named hooks are installed: one that merely exists changes the observed order.\nconst recordingHooks = (callOrder: string[], names: RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n      return msg;\n    },\n    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n      return systemPrompt;\n    },\n    afterModelResponse: async () => void callOrder.push('afterModelResponse'),\n    afterSession: async () => void callOrder.push('afterSession'),\n    onError: async () => void callOrder.push('onError'),\n  };\n\n  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n};\n\n// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\nconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n\n  return {\n    sent,\n    provider: {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        sent.systemPrompt = params.systemPrompt;\n        const last = params.messages[params.messages.length - 1];\n        sent.lastUserContent = typeof last.content === 'string' ? last.content : '';\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createRejectingProvider(reason: unknown): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw reason;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}","originalFile":"import {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, SendMessageParams, UsageMetadata} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\n\n// Fixed: nothing asserts on it, and new Date() would tie the run to the clock.\nconst SENT_AT = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';\n\n// One expect per test needs the rejection as a value rather than a matcher.\nconst outcomeOf = (call: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> =>\n  call.then(() => 'resolved without throwing').catch((error: Error) => error.message);\n\nfunction createSession(content: string): ChatSession {\n  return {\n    sessionId: 's1',\n    userId: 'u1',\n    entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: SENT_AT}],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createMockProvider(response: string, usage?: UsageMetadata): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    async *sendMessage(_params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      yield {type: 'text', text: response};\n      yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn', usage};\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ntype RecordedHook =\n  | 'beforeSession'\n  | 'beforeUserInput'\n  | 'afterUserInput'\n  | 'beforeModelResponse'\n  | 'afterModelResponse'\n  | 'afterSession'\n  | 'onError';\n\n// Only the named hooks are installed: one that merely exists changes the observed order.\nconst recordingHooks = (callOrder: string[], names: RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n      return msg;\n    },\n    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n      return systemPrompt;\n    },\n    afterModelResponse: async () => void callOrder.push('afterModelResponse'),\n    afterSession: async () => void callOrder.push('afterSession'),\n    onError: async () => void callOrder.push('onError'),\n  };\n\n  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n};\n\n// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\nconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n\n  return {\n    sent,\n    provider: {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        sent.systemPrompt = params.systemPrompt;\n        const last = params.messages[params.messages.length - 1];\n        sent.lastUserContent = typeof last.content === 'string' ? last.content : '';\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n    },\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator hooks', () => {\n  describe('beforeSession', () => {\n    it('called before anything else', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['beforeSession', 'beforeUserInput', 'beforeModelResponse']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder[0]).toBe('beforeSession');\n    });\n\n    it('runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      let onErrorMessage: string | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n          throw new Error('beforeSession boom');\n        },\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          callOrder.push('onError');\n          onErrorMessage = error.message;\n        },\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('never'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, onErrorMessage, callOrder}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'beforeSession boom',\n        onErrorMessage: 'beforeSession boom',\n        callOrder: ['beforeSession', 'onError', 'afterSession'],\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterSession', () => {\n    it('called after everything completes', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder).toEqual(['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n    });\n\n    it('called even when an error occurs', async () => {\n      let afterSessionCalled = false;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async () => {},\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          afterSessionCalled = true;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, afterSessionCalled}).toEqual({outcome: 'boom', afterSessionCalled: true});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('beforeUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives the last user message content', async () => {\n      let receivedMessage = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createRejectingProvider(reason: unknown): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw reason;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ntype RecordedHook =\n  | 'beforeSession'\n  | 'beforeUserInput'\n  | 'afterUserInput'\n  | 'beforeModelResponse'\n  | 'afterModelResponse'\n  | 'afterSession'\n  | 'onError';\n\n// Only the named hooks are installed: one that merely exists changes the observed order.\nconst recordingHooks = (callOrder: string[], names: RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n      return msg;\n    },\n    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n      return systemPrompt;\n    },\n    afterModelResponse: async () => void callOrder.push('afterModelResponse'),\n    afterSession: async () => void callOrder.push('afterSession'),\n    onError: async () => void callOrder.push('onError'),\n  };\n\n  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n};\n\n// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\nconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n\n  return {\n    sent,\n    provider: {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        sent.systemPrompt = params.systemPrompt;\n        const last = params.messages[params.messages.length - 1];\n        sent.lastUserContent = typeof last.content === 'string' ? last.content : '';\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n    },\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator hooks', () => {\n  describe('beforeSession', () => {\n    it('called before anything else', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['beforeSession', 'beforeUserInput', 'beforeModelResponse']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder[0]).toBe('beforeSession');\n    });\n\n    it('runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      let onErrorMessage: string | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n          throw new Error('beforeSession boom');\n        },\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          callOrder.push('onError');\n          onErrorMessage = error.message;\n        },\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('never'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, onErrorMessage, callOrder}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'beforeSession boom',\n        onErrorMessage: 'beforeSession boom',\n        callOrder: ['beforeSession', 'onError', 'afterSession'],\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterSession', () => {\n    it('called after everything completes', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder).toEqual(['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n    });\n\n    it('called even when an error occurs', async () => {\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await describe('beforeUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives the last user message content', async () => {\n      let receivedMessage = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_session, userMessage) => {\n          receivedMessage = userMessage;\n          return userMessage;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('my answer'));\n\n      expect(receivedMessage).toBe('my answer');\n    });\n\n    it('modifies user message when returning different value', async () => {\n      const {provider, sent} = capturingProvider();\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('raw input'));\n\n      expect(sent.lastUserContent).toBe('sanitized input');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives user unknown}).reason,\n      }).toEqual({\n        wasErrorCalled: true,\n        isErrorInstance: true,\n        message: 'string boom',\n        originalThrown: 'string boom',\n      });\n    });","originalFile":"import {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, SendMessageParams, UsageMetadata} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\n\n// Fixed: nothing asserts on it, and new Date() would tie the run to the clock.\nconst SENT_AT = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';\n\n// One expect per test needs the rejection as a value rather than a matcher.\nconst outcomeOf = (call: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> =>\n  call.then(() => 'resolved without throwing').catch((error: Error) => error.message);\n\nfunction createSession(content: string): ChatSession {\n  return {\n    sessionId: 's1',\n    userId: 'u1',\n    entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: SENT_AT}],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createMockProvider(response: string, usage?: UsageMetadata): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    async *sendMessage(_params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      yield {type: 'text', text: response};\n      yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn', usage};\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createRejectingProvider(reason: unknown): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw reason;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ntype RecordedHook =\n  | 'beforeSession'\n  | 'beforeUserInput'\n  | 'afterUserInput'\n  | 'beforeModelResponse'\n  | 'afterModelResponse'\n  | 'afterSession'\n  | 'onError';\n\n// Only the named hooks are installed: one that merely exists changes the observed order.\nconst recordingHooks = (callOrder: string[], names: RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n      return msg;\n    },\n    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n      return systemPrompt;\n    },\n    afterModelResponse: async () => void callOrder.push('afterModelResponse'),\n    afterSession: async () => void callOrder.push('afterSession'),\n    onError: async () => void callOrder.push('onError'),\n  };\n\n  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n};\n\n// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\nconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n\n  return {\n    sent,\n    provider: {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        sent.systemPrompt = params.systemPrompt;\n        const last = params.messages[params.messages.length - 1];\n        sent.lastUserContent = typeof last.content === 'string' ? last.content : '';\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n    },\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator hooks', () => {\n  describe('beforeSession', () => {\n    it('called before anything else', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['beforeSession', 'beforeUserInput', 'beforeModelResponse']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder[0]).toBe('beforeSession');\n    });\n\n    it('runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      let onErrorMessage: string | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n          throw new Error('beforeSession boom');\n        },\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          callOrder.push('onError');\n          onErrorMessage = error.message;\n        },\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('never'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, onErrorMessage, callOrder}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'beforeSession boom',\n        onErrorMessage: 'beforeSession boom',\n        callOrder: ['beforeSession', 'onError', 'afterSession'],\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterSession', () => {\n    it('called after everything completes', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder).toEqual(['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n    });\n\n    it('called even when an error occurs', async () => {\n      let afterSessionCalled = false;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async () => {},\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          afterSessionCalled = true;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, afterSessionCalled}).toEqual({outcome: 'boom', afterSessionCalled: true});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('beforeUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives the last user message content', async () => {\n      let receivedMessage = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_session, userMessage) => {\n          receivedMessage = userMessage;\n          return userMessage;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('my answer'));\n\n      expect(receivedMessage).toBe('my answer');\n    });\n\n    it('modifies user message when returning different value', async () => {\n      const {provider, sent} = capturingProvider();\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_session, _msg) => 'sanitized input',\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('raw input'));\n\n      expect(sent.lastUserContent).toBe('sanitized input');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives user message after any beforeUserInput modification', async () => {\n      let afterReceived = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_s, _msg) => 'modified',\n        afterUserInput: async (_s, msg) => {\n          afterReceived = msg;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('original'));\n\n      expect(afterReceived).toBe('modified');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('beforeModelResponse', () => {\n    it('replaces system prompt with returned value', async () => {\n      const {provider, sent} = capturingProvider();\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeModelResponse: async (_session, _basePrompt) => 'Custom prompt for this question',\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(sent.systemPrompt).toBe('Custom prompt for this question');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterModelResponse', () => {\n    it('receives collected response text and usage', async () => {\n      const usage: UsageMetadata = {inputTokens: 10, outputTokens: 5, totalTokens: 15};\n      let receivedText = '';\n      let receivedUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        afterModelResponse: async (_session, text, u) => {\n          receivedText = text;\n          receivedUsage = u;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hello world', usage), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hi'));\n\n      expect({receivedText, receivedUsage}).toEqual({receivedText: 'hello world', receivedUsage: usage});\n    });\n\n    it('not called when stream throws', async () => {\n      let afterCalled = false;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        afterModelResponse: async () => {\n          afterCalled = true;\n        },\n        onError: async () => {},\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n        createErrorProvider(new AIError('fail', 'TEST')),\n        promptBuilder,\n        undefined,\n        {hooks}\n      );\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, afterCalled}).toEqual({outcome: 'fail', afterCalled: false});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('onError', () => {\n    it('called with session and error when stream fails', async () => {\n      let receivedError: Error | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          receivedError = error;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n        createErrorProvider(new AIError('rate limited', 'RATE_LIMITED')),\n        promptBuilder,\n        undefined,\n        {hooks}\n      );\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, receivedErrorMessage: receivedError?.message}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'rate limited',\n        receivedErrorMessage: 'rate limited',\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('error still propagates after onError hook', async () => {\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async () => {},\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      await expect(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'))).rejects.toThrow('boom');\n    });\n\n    it('wraps a thrown string in an Error before calling onError', async () => {\n      let receivedError: Error | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          receivedError = error;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createRejectingProvider('string boom'), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({\n        outcome,\n        wasErrorCalled: receivedError !== undefined,\n        isErrorInstance: receivedError instanceof Error,\n        message: receivedError?.message,\n      }).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'string boom',\n        wasErrorCalled: true,\n        isErrorInstance: true,\n        message: 'string boom',\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('full lifecycle order', () => {\n    it('calls all hooks in correct order', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const usage: UsageMetadata = {inputTokens: 5, outputTokens: 3, totalTokens: 8};\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n        },\n        beforeUserInput: async (_s, msg) => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n          return msg;\n        },\n        afterUserInput: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterUserInput');\n        },\n        beforeModelResponse: async (_s, p) => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n          return p;\n
